2026. 10.27
先週（10/19～10/26）発表の主な経済指標
米生産活動は堅調。AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子
製品や機械などがけん引。一方、データセンター建設に伴う需要
増加が、他産業の生産や建設活動を圧迫する兆候も
コア消費者物価は再び2％超え。特殊要因を除くサービス価格
も加速し、人件費転嫁の兆し。連合は2027年春闘で6％中心
を要求。中小企業の余力に課題も、物価上振れに注意
注目点② サービス物価の加速で重み増す2027年春闘戦線注目点①AI投資の恩恵拡大、副作用もじわり顕在化
総合PMIは57.6と高水準を維持。新規失業保険申
請件数も19.4万件と低水準で解雇の動きは引き続
き限定的。一方で、住宅着工件数や住宅建設業
者の景況感は悪化、高金利の影響が継続

ベージュブック
（2026/7）
米国：AI関連を中心に製造業生産は堅調も、資材・設備の需給ひっ迫が他産業に波及
 米国の製造業生産は、AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子製品、一次金属、一般機械などが堅調
― 一方、非耐久財や家具など消費関連の生産は軟調に推移し、業種間のばらつきが拡大
 FRBは、データセンター建設に伴う鋼材・電気設備などの需要増加が、他の建設案件や生産活動に影響していると指摘
―AI投資の拡大が関連産業の生産を押し上げる一方、供給制約を通じて他産業の活動を圧迫する兆候も
― 当面はAI投資による成長押し上げ効果が期待される一方、供給制約や価格上昇を通じた負の影響にも注意が必要
4

日本：全国コアCPIは再び2％超え。基調的なサービス価格も伸び高まる
5
 9月の全国コアCPIは前年比＋2.1％（前月：同＋1.7％）と加速。2025年12月以来の2％超に
― 原油高・円安等のコスト上昇もあり、家事用消耗品や教養娯楽用品等を中心に伸び率が拡大
― 一般サービス価格も伸び加速。シルバーウィークの並びによる宿泊料や外食費の上昇などが主因
 特殊要因を除いた一般サービス価格もじわり上昇
― 変動の大きい宿泊費や私立高校無償化による押し下げを除いた基調的な一般サービス価格に上昇圧力。人件費増の
価格転嫁が進んでいる可能性を示唆

全国コアCPI CPI一般サービス価格の推移

### Page 7

日本：連合は2027年春闘で賃上げ要求を強化。高水準の賃上げ持続と物価への波及が焦点
6
 連合は、2027年春闘賃上げ要求の目安を「6％中心、5％以上」と発表
―5％以上の賃上げを最低目標として明確化。実質賃金1％上昇の定着をめざす方針
― 中小企業は大企業との格差是正に向け「6％以上」が目標。過去4年のインフレで実質賃金が低下した分の回復も要求
― また、新たに、2030年に2026年の賃金水準を15％程度上回る中期目標を設定。持続的な賃金上昇をめざす
 賃上げ余力は企業規模・業種で濃淡。中小企業等で高い「賃上げ5％の壁」
― なお、6％に近い賃上げが実現する場合、サービス価格への波及が強まり、基調的物価上昇率が2％を上回る可能性
連合・春闘基本構想の新旧比較 春闘賃上げ率

基本構想」 より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 8

欧州：2027年度予算審議が本格化。生活苦によるデモが拡大する中、財政赤字は高水準
7
 2027年度予算審議が本格化。財政赤字は総じて高止まり
― 一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連支出を柔軟化する特例措置の適用後も、複数の国でEU財政基準で定める赤字幅を超
過。スペインは予算案を巡って議会解散。フランス・ベルギーも10/15時点でEUに予算案を未提出
 生活改善を求める抗議活動が欧州各国で発生。支出圧力の高まりは財政再建の障壁に
― フランスの学生によるデモを発端に、生活費の高騰や公共サービスの劣化に対する抗議活動は欧州各国に拡大。一部
は暴動に発展し、警察官と衝突する事態に
9～10月に発生した主な抗議活動主要国の財政収支実績と見通し
（出所） 各種報道より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：金融市場は政治・財政を巡る混乱を嫌気。不透明感は2027年入り後も継続する見込み
8
 金融市場は政治・財政を巡る混乱を警戒。主要国の選挙を控える中、不透明感は2027年まで継続へ
― 抗議活動が広がるイタリア、フランス、ベルギーの一般政府債務残高（GDP比）は、EU加盟国の中でも上位（それぞれ2
位、3位、4位）。金利上昇の背景は政治・財政への懸念
― 影響は為替市場にも波及。9月中旬以降、ユーロはドルに対して弱含み

## Document 031: 2026-11-12_［みずほ経済インサイト］アジアでデータセンター投資が拡大_111294bf

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-12_［みずほ経済インサイト］アジアでデータセンター投資が拡大_111294bf.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

アジアでデータセンター投資が拡大 
供給制約への対応力が投資の持続性を左右

○ アジアでデータセンター投資が拡大している。その背景には、AI・クラウド需要の拡大や各国・
地域の投資誘致策、クラウド事業者の戦略、建設コストの低さなどが挙げられる 
○ データセンター投資が増加することは、総固定資本形成の増加や、半導体・サーバーなどの輸出
拡大、雇用創出、技術・知識の移転につながる可能性がある 
○ 今後も需要拡大が見込まれる一方、立地地域が抱える電力・水などのインフラ負荷は大きい。 
投資の経済効果と負荷とのバランスを踏まえた政策対応が、投資拡大の持続性を左右するだろう 
１．アジアでデータセンター投資が拡大 
アジアでデータセンター投資が拡大している。図表1は、同地域におけるデータセンター関連の対内
直接投資計画額を示したものである。データセンター投資は2024年頃から大きく増加し、2026年上半
期も高水準を維持している。主要な投資先であるASEAN向け投資が一段と拡大したほか、2025年以降は
インド、オーストラリアでも投資が急増している。 
また、アジアのFDI流入におけるデータセンターの存在感も高まっている。同地域の対内直接投資計
画額全体に占めるデータセンターの割合は、2025年に約30％まで上昇した（図表2）。 
図表 1 アジアのデータセンター直接投資計画額  図表 2 FDI 流入額中のデータセンター投資比率

２．アジアにおいてデータセンター投資がなぜ増加しているのか 
アジアでデータセンター投資が拡大している背景には、投資受入国におけるデジタル関連需要の増
加や、それを受けた投資誘致の活発化、また投資側企業にとってコスト面などから当該地域の相対的
な優位性が増していることが挙げられる。 
実際、投資受入国ではデジタルサービスに対する需要が拡大している。Gelvanovska-Garcia et al.
（2024）によれば、東アジア・太平洋地域では、インターネットの普及や経済のデジタル化を背景に、
製造業や医療など幅広い分野でクラウドサービスの導入が進んでいると指摘している。こうしたサー
ビスの利用拡大は、データの処理・保存を担うデータセンターの需要を高めることにつながる。 
また、各国政府がデータセンター誘致を活発化させていることも、アジアにおけるデータセンター
投資を後押ししている。図表3は、各国のデータセンター関連の優遇策を示したものだが、各国で誘致
を目的とした優遇政策がとられている。タイやインドでは、一定の条件を満たすデータセンター事業
や関連事業者に対して、所得税等の免税措置にまで踏み込んでいる。 
対して、投資側企業にとっては、需要地の近くにデータセンターを整備することによる、自社サー
ビスの品質向上が期待される1。事実、アジアにおける主要なデータセンター投資案件の企業発表にお
いても、アジアへの投資目的として、現地企業や政府への低遅延サービス提供、通信環境の強靭化な
どが挙げられている2。 
サービス品質向上に加え、ASEANやインド等における建設コストの低さも投資を後押しする要因とな
っている。図表4は、データセンターにおけるIT負荷容量1メガワットあたりの建設コストを比較した
ものだが、インドやベトナム、インドネシアなどはデータセンターの建設費用が相対的に小さく、シ
ンガポール等の地域に比べ、低コストでデータセンターの容量増強が可能である。

図表 3 データセンター誘致策の例 図表 4 データセンターの建設コスト

３．データセンター投資はアジア経済に何をもたらすのか 
データセンター投資の拡大は、アジア経済にどのような影響をもたらすのだろうか。主なポイント
は以下の4つである。 
まず第一に、総固定資本形成の押し上げである。データセンターの設置時、企業は建物を建設する
ほか、サーバーや受変電、冷却、通信等の設備を購入する。こうした企業活動は、GDP統計上では総固
定資本形成中の建設・機械設備投資として計上され、経済成長率の押し上げ要因となる3。実際、オー
ストラリアでは2025年以降、情報・通信部門の機械投資がGDP成長率を押し上げており、データセンタ
ー投資拡大の影響がうかがえる。 
また第二のポイントは、AI関連財の輸出拡大である。東アジアやASEANは、半導体やサーバー等の製
造拠点であり、製品需要の拡大は輸出の追い風となる。図表5に示す通り、アジアのAI関連財輸出はAI
ブームのもとで急拡大が続いている。とりわけ、2026年以降は中国などアジア域内向け輸出の増勢が
顕著であり、アジア域内におけるデータセンター設置拡大の影響がみてとれる。 
そして第三のポイントは、雇用の拡大である。データセンターの設置時に建設や設備設置の作業員
が必要となるほか、稼働後も設備管理やサーバー運用に関わる人材の雇用が創出される可能性がある。 
最後は、技術・知識の波及である。データセンターを運営する外国企業が現地企業と取引すること
により、外国企業から現地企業へ技術が伝わることが期待される。例えば、電力・冷却・通信設備の
設計や施工、保守管理にかかるノウハウを、現地企業が獲得するケースなどがある。 
ただし、経済波及効果の実現には不確実性が伴う点は留意する必要がある。中でも、雇用への波及
効果は限定的なものに留まる可能性がある。というのも、fDi Marketsが集計する投資計画データによ
ると（図表6）、海外直接投資100万米ドルあたりの直接雇用創出数は、データセンター投資の場合、全
産業平均よりも少数にとどまるからだ。 
図表 5 アジアのAI関連財輸出額（仕向け地別） 図表 6 海外直接投資100万米ドルあたりの 
直接雇用創出数（2022～2025年平均）

４．データセンター投資の先行きとリスク要因 
今後のデータセンター投資は、需要面での拡大余地が大きいと見込まれる一方、供給面では様々な
制約が表面化する可能性がある。 
まず、需要面については、クラウド需要の拡大がデータセンターの投資を促す公算が大きい。実際、
主要な民間調査会社の予想によれば、電子商取引やAI利活用の浸透によりデータの保存・処理需要が
増加し、アジアにおけるクラウド市場の拡大が続くとされている4。 
一方、先行きの供給制約となりうる要因も少なくない。具体的には、電力・水などの資源制約と、
政策変更の2つである。 
資源制約については、第一に電力供給の制約がある。アジアでは、今後も電力需要の拡大が見込ま
れる一方で、それに見合った電力供給の拡大が容易ではないからだ。図表7は、IEAが2026年2月に公表
した、2026～2030年の年平均増加率の予測を示している。掲載した各国・地域ではいずれも電力需要
の増加が見込まれ、特にインドやベトナムなどで高い伸びとなっていることがわかる。産業活動の拡
大や冷房需要の増加、データセンターの集積などが需要拡大をけん引する格好だ。 
一方、電力供給の伸びは、需要と比べ、緩やかなものにとどまる可能性が高い。IEAによれば、東南
アジアの電力生産量は2026～2030年に年平均5.2％の増加と、電力需要の伸び（年平均5.4％）を下回
り、電力需給のひっ迫が続くことが想定されている5。 
また、データセンターの立地が特定地域に集中しやすい点にも留意が必要だ6。特定地域で電力需給
がひっ迫した場合、送電網の不足などで他地域からの融通ができない場合がある7。 
資源制約については、電力のみならず、水の安定供給という課題もある。冷却に大量の水を使用す
るデータセンターでは、施設の新設・拡張に伴って水需要が増加すると見込まれるからだ。必要な水
量を確保できなければ、施設の稼働が制約されることになりかねない。ASEAN事務局（2025）は、デー
タセンターの冷却に伴う水需要の増加は、農業・工業・住民生活向けの水利用と競合する可能性があ
ると指摘している8。 
二点目は、政策変更のリスクである。雇用拡大等
を通じた地場経済への貢献度の低さや、資源利用や
安全性をめぐる地域住民からの反発などを背景に、
投資許認可の厳格化や新規開発の制限が行われる
可能性がある。 
地場経済への貢献については、先述の通り、デー
タセンターの直接的な雇用創出効果は他の投資に
比べて限られる。その結果、受入国・地域が期待し
ていた経済効果よりも現地経済への貢献が小さい
となった場合、投資誘致や優遇税制の見直しにつな
がる可能性は否定できない。また、地域住民からの
反発については、図表8で示す通り、既にマレーシ
ア、インドネシア、インド、オーストラリアなどで、
水利用や生活環境への影響などを巡り、データセン

ター投資への抗議や反対運動が起きている。 
こうした状況のもと、一部の国ではすでにデータセンター投資関連の規制を見直す動きが広がって
いる。実際にタイでは、政府による委員会組織（データセンタービジネス委員会）が、データセンタ
ー166件の建設や許可手続きを一時停止した。停止の理由としては、資源の利用、立地、安全性に関す
る全国的な基準の策定が必要であることが挙げられている9。タイ政府は投資誘致を維持すると示して
いるものの、予見可能性の低下から、進捗中案件の見直しや計画中案件の中止につながるリスクは完
全に拭えない。

５．供給制約への対応力がデータセンター投資の行方を左右 
アジアでは、AI・クラウド需要の拡大を背景に、今後もデータセンター投資の増加が続くと見込ま
れる。ただし、巨額の投資に比べて雇用創出効果は限定的とみられる一方、電力・水などのインフラ
負荷は大きく、投資の拡大が必ずしも受入国・地域に十分な経済的便益をもたらすとは限らない。 
こうした中、今後のデータセンター投資では、建設コストの低さや税制優遇に加え、電力・水の安
定供給や規制・許認可の予見可能性などが、投資先を選別する上で一段と重要になると考えられる。
供給制約が深刻化する地域では、投資計画の遅延や縮小が生じる一方、安定的な事業環境を確保でき
る地域に投資が集中する可能性もある。 
受入国にとっても、単に投資額の拡大を追求するのではなく、国内産業への波及やインフラ整備の
負担を見極めることが重要となる。今後のアジアにおけるデータセンター投資は、需要拡大が成長を
支える一方で、供給制約への対応力の違いが、各国・地域のへの投資配分を左右することになるだろ
う。

図表 8 データセンターを巡る抗議活動の例

電網容量を大幅に増強する必要が生じる可能性があること、また電力供給が途切れないよう安定的な電力網を構築する必要があ
ることを指摘している。 
7 ベトナム北部では、2023 年 5～6 月に断続的な停電が発生し、多くの家庭や企業に影響が広がった。World Bank（2023）
“Taking Stock, August 2023: Making Public Investment Work for Growth”は、送電網整備の遅れにより、南部の余剰発電

8 ASEAN（2025）“ASEAN Guide for Sustainable Data Centre Development”によれば、データセンターの中でもハイパースケ
ール施設の冷却システムでは、水使用量が 1 日当たり最大 150 万リットルに達する場合があり、干ばつが発生しやすい地域の水
供給や生態系に大きな負荷を与えうると説明している。また、水利用の効率化を促す政策的枠組みがなければ、データセンター
の水需要が、農業・工業・地域住民に必要な水の確保を圧迫する可能性を指摘している。

市場焦點
期中選舉
• 美國期中選舉後，市場擔憂民主黨可能放緩AI資料中心的建設並提高對AI的監管，促使科技股
面臨估值修正壓力。但從基本面觀察，2026年標普500企業獲利年增率預估已由7月的26.5%
大幅上修至36.1%，其中資訊科技產業預估成長64.5%，通訊服務亦有52.0%。大型科技企業
仍具備穩健現金流與持續投入AI基礎建設的能力，AI長期發展趨勢尚未改變，產業基本面不宜
因短期估值修正而過度看淡。
中國A股
• 短期來看A股反彈行情有機會延續，主因為美國期中選舉落幕、中國政策環境偏寬鬆，以及A
股估值與情緒指標落在歷史相對低檔，具備超跌反彈條件，政策面方面，人行可能進一步降息，
政策利率由1.25%下調至1%，搭配IPO超募資金回購、穩定資金加大ETF買入力道，有助於改

善市場流動性並提振投資信心。
石油/黃金
• 波斯灣國家能源出口替代方案已略見成效，不過由於替代路徑運輸成本較高，能源價格仍居
高不下，觀察過去幾週出口及價格變化，推測在美伊關係未有明確變化的情境下，國際油價
將在每桶100美元附近震盪。
• 央行購金需求維持高檔，但相較去年同期累計未進一步放大。在此情境下，短中期黃金價格
能否進一步突破的關鍵，可能取決於聯準會貨幣政策的調整空間。

• 選民不滿情緒發酵，民主黨在期中選舉取得重大突破：伊朗局勢久拖不決、通膨持續攀升，削弱選民對川普政府的支持，民
主黨因此在期中選舉中於參、眾兩院取得過半席次，新一屆國會將在2027年1月3日就職，進入白宮與國會分由不同政黨掌
控的「分裂政府」時代。州長選戰方面，民主黨也成功翻轉多個原由共和黨長期執政的州，州長席次占比睽違20年再度超越
共和黨，其中德州、喬治亞州、俄亥俄州及愛荷華州的選情翻轉尤其受到矚目。
• 國會制衡與州政府版圖變化，政策走向仍存不確定性： 儘管民主黨成功奪回眾議院，但在參議院尚未取得足以單獨推動重大
法案的關鍵席次，例如除了財政預算相關法案可以用預算調節程序簡單多數通過外，一般的法案都需要60票才能通過，因此
未來重大政策仍須仰賴跨黨協商，市場對政策劇烈轉向的疑慮可望有所緩解。不過，民主黨掌握更多州政府，也可能促使部

分州在川普剩餘兩年任期內，進一步制衡或抵制聯邦施政議程，並對2028年總統大選的選務布局產生重要影響。
美國民主黨贏得聯邦參議院、眾議院以及德克薩斯州州長選舉

• 美國經濟具韌性，紮根美股未來底氣：美國期中選舉後，市場擔憂民主黨可能放
緩AI資料中心的建設並提高對AI的監管，促使科技股面臨估值修正壓力。但從基
本面觀察，標普500企業獲利展望仍持續改善。2026年標普500企業獲利年增率

鏈；然而，大型科技企業仍具備穩健現金流與持續投入AI基礎建設的能力，AI長
期發展趨勢尚未改變，產業基本面不宜因短期估值修正而過度看淡。

回
顧
美國政治藍色浪潮來襲：AI數據中心建設受阻與產業鏈資產重估
• 政治監管與地方風向轉變：AI基礎設施擴建面臨停工與審查的壓力，民主黨在多個關鍵州分贏得州長選舉，並伴隨地方公投
限制措施的升溫，使得原本蓬勃發展的AI數據中心建設計畫大幅放緩。
• 關鍵州分政策轉嚴：德州、喬治亞州與俄亥俄州等AI數據中心投資重鎮，當選民主黨州長普遍對數據中心開發採取強硬立場。
德州喊出暫停新建並取消稅收優惠，喬治亞州提出全州暫緩新項目，俄亥俄州與威斯康辛州亦對能源消耗、環境審查及稅收
補貼提出嚴格管制條件。
• 地方公投與審查週期拉長：全美46項涉及數據中心的地方限制措施中，高達40項趨向收緊或已通過更嚴格限制，未來開發
項目將面臨更長審批週期、更嚴格電網接入審查及更高公用事業與監管合規成本。

• 未成熟開發商延期風險高：以全美儲備規模最大的德州為例，超過半數項目尚處於早期階段，且大量由缺乏營運經驗的初次
開發商主導。在監管與水電資源收緊下，此類項目極易面臨延期或取消的風險。
AI建設熱潮面臨民主黨藍色浪潮衝擊 46項地方數據中心措施投票結果分布
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.05

美國製造業景氣加速擴張，原物料成本壓力仍牽制通膨降溫
• 製造業擴張動能增強，生產與新訂單同步改善：美國10月ISM製造業PMI升至55.5，較9月54.5增加1%，連續第10個月
維持擴張；其中生產指數升至59.0，創2021年11月以來新高，新訂單指數亦升至55.5，顯示製造業接單及生產活動持
續增溫，且存貨指數回升至51.4，反映企業庫存由收縮轉為擴張，製造業景氣復甦動能進一步增強。
• 需求回升伴隨成本壓力，通膨降溫仍面臨挑戰：供應商交貨指數升至59.1，代表交貨速度持續放緩，連續11個月呈現相
同趨勢；客戶端庫存指數維持41.6，顯示庫存水位仍偏低，有利後續補庫存需求。然而，原物料價格指數雖由77.9降至
75.2，仍連續第25個月處於上漲區間，反映鋼鋁、石化及進口商品成本壓力尚未消退，可能使通膨降溫進程更加緩慢。

• 後市觀察：美國製造業景氣持續擴張，反映企業需求及經濟活動仍具韌性，有助支撐企業營收與獲利；惟原物料價格維
持高檔，可能增加通膨黏著性，使聯準會貨幣政策維持審慎，並對美債殖利率形成支撐。後續市場將關注製造業需求能
否延續，以及成本壓力是否逐步傳導至終端物價。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.11.3

資料來源：ISM Manufacturing PMI Report， 2026.11.3

企業財務與行業分化：高槓桿新雲業者受創，巨頭發揮定價優勢
• 政策與審查趨嚴使得整體AI建設成本上揚，但在產業內卻呈現出極端的強者恆強、弱者面臨淘汰的兩極趨勢。
• 新雲公司(Neoclouds)面臨流動性與違約風險：若數據中心建設延誤或無法如期接入電網，收入推遲產生，而這類新興雲端
服務商普遍具高財務槓桿，高額利息費用持續累積，導致現金流期限錯配，將提高其再融資風險、擴大信用利差，甚至觸發
合約中的不可抗力條款與債務違約風險。
• 超大規模雲端巨頭(Hyperscalers)享受供應趨緊紅利：對科技巨頭而言，基礎設施擴建受阻反而是利多。已擁有現成數據中
心與電力保障的巨頭定價權大幅提升，算力供給緊俏能將雲端計算服務價格維持在高檔。此外，巨頭有能力自行籌資進行專
用發電或直接與公用事業公司談判，進一步拉開與新進入者的差距。

• AI六大支柱亮警報：支撐AI熱潮持續發展六大條件(訂單積壓、融資、算力需求、競爭、政治、聯準會)中，政治、競爭及聯
準會三項已現疲軟，特別是詞元(Token)成本持續下降、模型可替代性提高，低價模型加劇競爭，持續擠壓軟體獲利空間。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.05
詞元(Token)成本持續下降• 小結與投資建議：期中選舉引發的政策變革，正將AI
行業從無差別大舉擴建帶入嚴格監管與高成本的新階
段。供給側瓶頸(電網、土地、監管)將淘汰財務體質孱
弱、高度依賴外部融資的高槓桿新興開發商；供給受
限也鞏固已具備規模與資源保障的雲端巨頭壟斷優勢
與定價權。 投資建議避開高槓桿新雲與中小型開發商，
聚焦資本雄厚且自備水電資源的超大規模雲端巨頭，
密切關注聯準會貨幣政策與信用利差變化，AI行業的
金融條件正在收緊，若聯準會降息放慢或信用利差持
續擴大，AI供應鏈中的高估值軟體與硬體股可能面臨
新一輪估值修正。

AI資本支出持續攀升，現金流承壓加劇企業信用分化
• AI基礎建設投資擴大，企業融資需求同步升溫：AI競賽已由
模型及技術開發延伸至資料中心、運算晶片與電力基礎設施
建置，帶動大型科技企業持續提高資本支出。Microsoft、
Alphabet、Amazon等企業近期公布的財報均顯示，AI相關
投資已進入高強度建置階段，市場預估大型科技企業2026年
AI基礎建設投資將超過7,000億美元，龐大資金需求也使市場
更加關注企業融資成本、資本配置效率及未來現金流表現。
• 財報反映投資成本，現金流與信用評等成市場檢驗重點：Q3
財報顯示，Alphabet及Amazon均因AI基礎建設投資大幅增
加，使自由現金流承受壓力；其中Amazon近12個月自由現
金流轉為負值，Alphabet單季亦出現負自由現金流。相較之

下，Microsoft仍維持正向自由現金流，反映企業財務承受能
力有所差異。此外，Meta因AI資本支出規模擴大及未來現金
流不確定性，遭標普全球調降信用評等展望至負向，顯示市
場已開始將AI投資效益納入企業信用風險評估。
• 市場看法：AI長期需求仍具成長潛力，但市場評價將逐步由
資本支出規模轉向投資報酬率、自由現金流及財務槓桿管理。
而具備穩定營運現金流、較高資本回收效率及明確AI商業化
成果的企業，較有能力維持投資動能；反之，若資本支出持
續攀升而營收及獲利未能同步跟上，將增加企業融資需求與
信用風險，並使科技企業間的評價差異進一步擴大。
大型科技企業擴大資本支出，承諾規模持續攀升
AI產業表外承諾大幅揚升
資料來源：Morgan Stanley Research，企業10-Q、10-K、8-K申報文件，ZeroHedge，
兆豐銀行彙整。

A股短期反彈行情可望延續，長期聚焦科技成長
• A股短期反彈行情可望延續，長期聚焦科技成長：從A股內部條件來看，
市場經歷長時間修正後，估值與情緒指標已降至歷史低檔，創業板本
益比回落至約26.5%，全市場站上200日均線個股占比僅約一成，顯示
悲觀情緒已相當充分反映，搭配市場成交熱度回升，盤面觀察近期銀
行、消費等權值板塊逐漸止穩，科技成長類股也開始出現籌碼與評價
修復，顯示市場輪動條件逐步成熟。短期而言，A股反彈行情仍有機會
延續，主因為美國期中選舉落幕、中國政策環境偏寬鬆，以及A股估值
與情緒指標落在歷史相對低檔，具備超跌反彈條件，政策面方面，人
行可能進一步降息，政策利率由1.25%下調至1%，搭配IPO超募資金
回購、穩定資金加大ETF買入力道，有助於改善市場流動性並提振投資

信心。
• 投資建議：操作上可優先布局半導體、AI硬體、通訊設備、電子零組
件、醫藥及機器人等具成長題材的族群，並留意商業航太等新興應用，
不過，反彈過程仍須觀察政策落實程度、企業獲利修復及海外市場變
化，中長期而言，A股仍以科技創新與科技服務實體經濟為核心主軸，
相關政策方向並未改變，建議維持科技成長為核心配置，搭配具景氣
復甦潛力的週期與消費產業，採取分批布局。
各指數估值與情緒指標已降至歷史低檔

中國10月製造業PMI跌回收縮區間，產需動能同步降溫
• 中國10月製造業PMI跌回收縮區間，產需動能同步降溫：中國10月製造業PMI降至49.3%，低於市場預期，也跌破50榮枯線，
顯示製造業景氣再度轉弱，主要受到十一長假前需求提前釋放，以及前期海外聖誕訂單集中出貨後進入調整期影響，使需求與
生產動能同步放緩，新訂單指數和生產指數皆下降，顯示企業生產活動亦有所收斂，不過若與去年同期相比，新訂單與生產指
數分別增加0.6及0.1個百分點，顯示製造業基本面仍具一定韌性，景氣表現優於去年同期，此外企業對未來仍維持審慎樂觀態
度，生產經營活動預期指數為53.4%，雖較前月略降，但仍位於擴張區間。
• 展望後市：隨著專項債加速投放，及8,000億元政策性金融工具逐步轉化為實體投資，基建及製造業需求有望持續釋放，帶動裝

備製造、鋼鐵等產業鏈景氣改善，此外11月為企業衝刺全年營運目標的重要時點，訂單接單與生產活動將較10月回溫，產業表
現方面，科技業因受惠於政策扶持及產業升級趨勢，維持強勁動能，消費品製造業則有望受雙十一購物帶動成長。

民主黨主張監管政策 間接支撐黃金價格表現
• 期中選舉為黃金價格帶來短期支撐：民主黨主導國會加深兩黨政治僵局的擔憂，加上可能推動更嚴格的AI產業監管，市場擔
憂對美國經濟成長帶來負面影響，進而增加聯準會採取寬鬆貨幣政策的可能性，同時美元走貶也是上週支撐金價的因素之一。
• 央行需求基本面穩健：世界黃金協會最新報告指出，各國央行在9月持續增加黃金持有量，當月淨購買量達61噸，為今年迄
今最高的單月總量。同時數據顯示，全球各國央行所申報的黃金淨購買量為228噸，較去年同期下降 7%。
• 觀點：央行購金需求維持高檔，但相較去年同期累計未進一步放大，同時高水準的美債殖利率排擠黃金的避險需求。在此情
境下，短中期黃金價格能否進一步突破的關鍵，可能取決於聯準會貨幣政策的調整空間。雖然市場大致排除激進升息的預期，

但緩步升息仍是基本情境，預料近期黃金價格將在每盎司4,000~4,200震盪。消息面來看，美伊衝突維持僵局，不過若是荷
姆茲海峽議題能有重大突破，將為黃金帶來進一步上漲空間。
資料來源：世界黃金協會，資料統計至9月底，2026.11.4。

期中選舉牽動，上週國際油價大幅震盪
• 選舉結果令國際油價大跌：選舉結果出爐後，市場預期民主黨將在預算與軍事決策上牽制總統川普，促使中東僵局加速透過
外交協商落幕。結果公布當天布蘭特油價單日大跌約9%，至每桶94美元，臨近週末則小幅反彈。上週油價整體收跌約16%，
主要反映選舉風險溢價消退，不過美伊僵局未變，油價仍在100美元附近震盪。
• 中東原油出口接近戰前水準，但成品油出口仍大幅落後：波斯灣國家正致力原油出口替代方案，目標擺脫荷姆茲海峽航運依
賴。石油出口量近幾週雖有所回落，但已接近戰前平均水準；然而，成品油出口動能恢復緩慢，目前仍遠不及戰前平均，這
也是造成全球能源通膨的主要原因。
• 觀點：波斯灣國家能源出口替代方案已略見成效，不過由於替代路徑運輸成本較高，能源價格仍居高不下，觀察過去幾週出

口及價格變化，推測在美伊關係未有明確變化的情境下，國際油價將在每桶100美元附近震盪。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.11.5。 資料來源：Bloomberg，兆豐銀行整理，2026.11.6。

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。

聯強(2347)：資金成本上揚壓抑短期營收動能，但長期營益率改善趨勢
不變 
建議：維持買進

一、聯強為亞太地區最大 3C 通
路商，營收成長以半導體業務最
佳  
 聯強為 3C 電子產品之銷售、配送、維修、量身訂做即時生產之專業通路商，為
亞太地區 3C 通路商龍頭，2026 年前三季產品比重分別為商用加值佔 31%、資
訊消費佔 17% 、 半 導 體 佔 48% 、 手 機 產 品 佔 4% ，成長主要來商用加值
(+112%YoY)和半導體業務(+162%YoY)。聯強海外市場包含中國、香港、紐澳、
泰國、印尼、印度及越南等地，其中以中國、台灣和印尼等區域較佳，營收分別
為 YoY+109%、YoY+78%和 YoY+18%。聯強競爭優勢為透過其四合一經營模
式，搭配其綿密的通路發揮規模經濟，並提供高附加價值的後勤運籌(庫存管理
與快速維為修服務等)，於中國主要競爭對手為神州數碼、天音通信控股。

二、半導體業務成長性佳，3Q26 
EPS 2.93 元創新高並優於預期 
 3Q26 營收 2366 億元(+18%QoQ；+136%YoY)優於預估值 14%，其中商用加
值和半導體營收分別為+116%YOY 和+273%YoY，因秉棄部分利潤不佳專案，
毛利率 3.49%優於前季之 3.4%，營業利益 61.41 億元(+28%%QoQ)優於預估
值 24%，因營運資金需求以及籌資成本上升致使利息費用提高至 9.34 億元
(+106%QoQ)，稅後純益 48.83 億元(+25%QoQ；+90%YoY)，EPS 2.93 元優
於預期之 2.5 元並創新高。

三、資金成本上揚導致業務拓展
較為謹慎，估計 4Q26 EPS 2.34
元 
 由於資金成本上揚致使公司放棄部分利潤不佳半導體業務，十月營收月減 34%，
估計 4Q26 營收 2046 億元(-14%QoQ；YoY+62%)，因產品組合變化轉佳，毛
利率提升至 3.58%，稅後純益 38.99 億元(-20%QoQ；+41%YoY)，EPS 2.34
元。

四、AI 長線需求有助商用加值和
半導體業務成長性 
 集邦 預估 2026 和 2027 年全 球九 大 CSP 資 本支 出分 別為 0.95 兆 美元
(+106%YoY)和 1.53 兆美元(+60%YoY)，且 AI 需求從雲端擴展到企業端，其中
以商用加值和半導體業務受惠幅度最大。 
在商用加值業務方面，在台灣和中國資料中心產品出貨挹注下，3Q26 營收
+116%YoY。展望 2027 年，中國十十五規劃和國產化政策推動政府和國企加速
採用本土品牌，推升聯強代理華為、浪潮等品牌業務成長性，台灣則是接獲數個
半導體大廠 AI 基礎建設專案，加計中小企業 AI 建置需求逐步顯現，商用業務將
為營運成長主要動能之一。 
在半導體業務方面，缺貨和漲價效致使記憶體、SSD 和硬碟業務為半導體業務
中 成 長 性 最 佳 者 ， 且 AI 推 論 亦 增 加 CPU 需求，3Q26 半 導 體 業 務 營 收

3Q26 遇到資金成本上揚，致使利息費用較前季倍增，公司將透過聯貸、可轉債

30%。展望 2027 年，儘管營收成長性不如 2026 年亮眼，但接單策略轉趨謹慎
將致使毛利率下滑壓力降低，且預期營業費用增幅有限下，營益率持續好轉方向
不變。

## Document 126: 261110_daiwa_hon-precision-buy-initiation

Source PDF: all report pdf/261110_daiwa_hon-precision-buy-initiation.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 23
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

Investment case: We initiate coverage of Hon Precision, the global leader 
in test handlers and active thermal control (ATC) systems, with a Buy (1) 
rating. According to management, the company commands >70% market 
share in AI/HPC final test (FT) and c.50% in system-level test (SLT) by 
revenue, distinctly positioning it as one of the key global beneficiaries of the 
rising silicon complexity.

Multi-layer content stacking supports ASP and margin growth. As test 
durations continue to lengthen and thermal requirements rise, customers 
are required to deploy greater parallel testing capacity and higher-
specification handlers, creating a structural demand tailwind for both FT 
and SLT handler. We forecast rising AI chip power consumption and 
package complexity over our forecast horizon to drive adoption of higher-
value ATC systems, mega-tray handlers (adding 25-30% to ASP relative to 
standard dual-temp ATC systems), and AOI integration (adding a further 
10-15% to system ASP), supporting a multi-year increase in content per 
tool and expanding its gross margin from 56.8% to 59.2% over 2026-28E.

Recurring consumables and CPO testing expand long-term growth 
visibility. Cold plates generate high-margin, annuity-like revenue with 
every new chip platform which should benefit from shortening AI processor 
refresh cycles. Beyond consumables, Hon Precision is extending its testing 
platform into co-packaged optics (CPO), where optical-electrical co-testing 
could unlock a new addressable market beyond traditional GPU and ASIC 
testing. While near-term contribution remains limited, we view CPO as a 
strategic growth option that enhances the company’s long-term earnings 
visibility and valuation premium over our forecast horizon.

Catalysts: 1) capacity expansion ramp in 2027; 2) mega-tray penetration 
and higher-power ATC adoption; 3) initial CPO test solution qualification.

Valuation: We initiate with a Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied on our 2027E EPS. The stock 
currently trades at c.19x forward PER, despite our projected 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 55%, implying a PEG of only 0.35x. We believe its current 
valuation heavily discounts macro noise with share price pulling back by 
c.53% from its historical high and derating from a cycle-peak multiple of 45-
50x 1-year-forward PER. Backed by solid order visibility through late-2027, 
we highlight Hon Precision’s risk-reward profile as decisively asymmetric.

Risks: 1) Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment; 2) delayed ramp-up of 
new products; and 3) potential loss of market share.

10 November 2026

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

How do we justify our view? 
Growth outlook Valuation Earnings revisions

Growth outlook

Hon Precision: revenue and gross margin outlook 
We expect Hon Precision’s growth momentum over 2026-
28E to be driven by increasing testing content per chip, 
supported by rising power consumption in AI/HPC chips, 
larger package sizes, and growing system complexity. We 
expect the transition towards high-end ATC handlers, 
mega-tray solutions, and integrated AOI functions to drive 
meaningful ASP expansion over our forecast horizon, with 
recurring cold plate demand providing an additional gross-
margin growth driver.

Source: Company, Daiwa research

Valuation   Hon Precision: 1-year-forward PER bands 
Our 12-month TP of TWD6,365 is based on a target PER 
of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. Following the 
strong share-price rally in 1H26, investor focus shifted 
away from Hon Precision due to limited near-term catalysts 
ahead of the 2H27 ramp-up of mega-tray handlers, CPO 
testing solutions and new capacity. We believe the recent 
53% pullback from its historical high on 29 May 2026 and 
derating from 45-50x 1-year-forward PER to c.19x more 
than prices in the macro uncertainties. At the current levels, 
the stock offers an attractive risk-reward profile, trading at 
a PEG of just 0.35x based on our 2026-28E EPS CAGR of 
55%.

Source: Bloomberg

Earnings revisions

Hon Precision: Bloomberg-consensus earnings revisions 
The Bloomberg consensus raised its 2026E and 2027E 
EPS estimates by 68% and 122%, respectively, over the 
past year, driven by stronger-than-expected AI-related 
demand and improving visibility on the company’s long-
term growth trajectory. We believe the upward revisions 
reflect rising confidence in volume and ASP expansion 
across the company’s key testing solutions, supported by 
accelerating AI infrastructure investments and expanding 
customer adoption.

Source: Bloomberg

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Hon Precision is the global leader in semiconductor test handlers and active thermal 
control (ATC) systems. The company dominates the global AI/HPC final test handler market, supported 
by its advanced thermal management technologies. With over 70% market share in AI/HPC test 
handling, Hon Precision benefits from growing demand for AI, HPC and custom ASIC applications. Its 
solutions are widely adopted by leading OSATs and foundries worldwide, making it a key enabler of 
advanced semiconductor testing.

### Page 6

6

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Growth Catalysts 
Multi-factor dollar content stacking 
We believe the primary growth catalyst for Hon Precision over our forecast horizon stems 
from multi-layered dollar content stacking, where the rise in machine unit value should 
outpace volume growth. Next-gen AI accelerators have historically exhibited more than 50-
70% increase in thermal design power (TDP) per generation, with platforms like Nvidia 
Rubin and Rubin Ultra surging to 2.3kW and 3.6kW vs. Blackwell’s 1.2kW to 1.4kW. 
Managing these extreme thermal loads makes high-end active thermal control (ATC) a 
necessity, driving a structural step-up in ASPs from standard handlers at approximately 
TWD6m to dual-temperature ATC systems at TWD12-15m, and tri-temperature ATC 
systems at TWD15-18m.

Concurrently, as the package size of next-generation ASICs/GPUs increases from the 
current 120mm tray specification to around 150mm, Hon Precision is introducing mega-
tray large-package solutions in October 2026, which command a 25-30% ASP premium 
over mainstream GPU handlers. As the majority of next-generation ASIC/GPU is set to 
exceed the boundary of 120mm, we estimate shipment penetration for mega-tray solutions 
will likely reach 40% in 2027E. Integrated functions of Automated Optical Inspection (AOI) 
will contribute an additional 10-15% ASP uplift to unit value of handler. Furthermore, this 
content expansion extends into the flagship mobile AP market as smartphone processors 
transition to multi-die packaging. For example, Apple’s A20 Pro processor has already 
adopted wafer-level multi-chip module (WMCM) packaging in 2026, requiring high-end ATC 
systems capable of managing up to 3,000W thermal dissipation to resolve device self-
heating.

Hon Precision: ASP multipliers

Source: Daiwa research

High-margin cold plate consumables  
In addition to capital equipment set sales, Hon Precision’s cold plate consumables 
business serves as a crucial recurring revenue engine and a primary driver of gross margin 
expansion. Cold plates carry the highest gross margin across all company product lines. 
Driven by the compression of AI processor lifecycles to 1-1.5 years (down from 2-3 years 
historically), each test handler requires 5-10 distinct, custom-engineered cold plates 
tailored to specific die footprints, socket layouts, and thermal hot-spot geometries. To 
conduct parallel test and maintain the throughput of chip testing, test handler for system 
level test (SLT) requires even more cold plates, which can go up to 24 plates. As a result, 
cold plate tooling generates high-frequency annuity-like sales that recur with every new 
chip platform iteration, independent of major equipment replacements.

Testing duration and volume multiples 
We believe the rapid rise in AI chip complexity is driving structural growth in semiconductor 
testing intensity, positioning Hon Precision as a key global beneficiary through its 
leadership in high-parallelism test handlers. Driven by the mainstream adoption of 
heterogeneous integration (such as TSMC’s CoWoS and Intel’s EMIB), verifying complex 
AI silicon has extended test durations by 2x to 10x vs. standard consumer chips (5 to 10 
seconds). Final test durations for AI GPUs have expanded from hundreds of seconds to 
over 10 minutes per chip. To maintain daily manufacturing throughput despite this 10x 
spike, chipmakers must scale up massive parallel testing architectures and procure 
significantly more handlers, cementing Hon Precision as the primary test handler partner 
for tier-one US AI GPU and CPU players.

Concurrently, escalating chip complexity, TDP, and die costs make field return (RMA) 
penalties far exceed testing expenses, forcing cloud service providers (CSPs) and 
automotive OEMs to mandate system-level testing (SLT). Because SLT executes over 
extended durations ranging from minutes to hours per chip, high-volume production 
mandates automated parallel SLT handlers equipped with high-capacity ATC. Holding 
c.50% market share in AI/HPC SLT handlers alongside Chroma, Hon Precision is 
expanding beyond GPUs into custom CSP ASICs (eg, Google TPU and AWS) and server 
CPUs. As SLT adoption broadens across non-GPU devices, Hon Precision leverages this 
multi-year runway to transition into a full-spectrum semiconductor testing platform.

Global: growing chip complexity – testing time by AI/HPC chip

Source: Daiwa research

CPO unlocks new TAM beyond GPUs 
Beyond conventional electrical testing, Co-Packaged Optics (CPO) represents a multi-year 
paradigm shift towards a multimodal optical-electrical testing platform. Hon Precision 
currently monetises initial CPO deployments by supplying Final Test (FT) handlers for pure 
electrical testing (E/E test) of switch ASICs and Optical Engines (OEs) on production 
platforms such as Nvidia’s Spectrum-X and Quantum-X.

### Page 8

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

However, the strategic growth catalyst is anchored in next-generation Insertion 4 (package-
level optical and electrical co-testing, O/E co-test). Because an undetected defect in a 
single optical component would force manufacturers to scrap an entire high-value CPO 
module, early and rigorous package-level validation is imperative for yield protection. 
Collaborating directly with ATE leader Teradyne and optical instrument specialists (such as 
Keysight), Hon Precision is developing a specialised ATC handler capable of simultaneous 
optical and electrical validation, including Bit Error Rate (BER), optical loopback, crosstalk, 
and high-power thermal management testing for integrated ASIC and Optical Engine 
modules in a single touchdown.

Engaged with Nvidia CPO switch programmes and actively expanding to additional ASIC 
clients, Hon Precision targets engineering verification in late-2026 and initial shipments in 
2027, ahead of 2028 volume production. Although we expect revenue contribution will 
likely be relatively low (<5% in 2027-2028), it drives valuation and market expectation to 
serve larger total addressable market (TAM).

Market Analysis 
Global testing equipment market 
According to TechInsights, global testing equipment capex is projected to expand 
significantly from USD8.3bn in 2024 to USD26.1bn by 2027E, representing a 46.4% 
CAGR. Notably, while SoC test systems front-loaded strong growth in 2024-25, before 
tapering to 9.6% YoY in 2027E on TechInsights estimates, package handling equipment is 
forecasted to exhibit strong structural resilience and longevity. Based on TechInsights, 
market capex for package handling equipment will likely more than double from USD941m 
in 2024 to USD2.6bn by 2027E (40.9% CAGR), re-accelerating to 46% YoY growth in 
2027E from 34% YoY in 2025. This cadence reflects the phased deployment of test 
infrastructure – initial tester investments are followed by high-volume back-end handling 
installations as advanced packaging (eg, CoWoS, Chiplets) scales into mass production.

Global semi testing equipment: capex  Global semi testing equipment: handler and prober growth

Source: TechInsights, Daiwa research  Source: TechInsights, Daiwa research

Handler market players 
Hon Precision’s overall share in the global test handler TAM rose from 35% in 2024 to 57% 
in 2025, and we forecast this to reach 75% in 2026E and 79% by 2027E. Within the high-
value AI and HPC Final Test (FT) handler segment, Hon Precision maintains a dominant 
market share, exceeding 70% in both sales value and shipment volume, per management. 
In the rapidly expanding AI and HPC SLT handler segment, Hon Precision holds c.50% 
market share in terms of revenue, per management, alongside its primary peer, Chroma, 
establishing a duopoly in global high-end SLT testing.

Hon Precision: handler market share forecasts

Source: Daiwa research

Outside of AI and HPC logic testing, the broader test handler landscape comprises global 
and regional vendors, including Cohu (COHU US, NR), Advantest (6857 JP, JPY28,375, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Daiwa analyst Takeshi Fukuyama), and mainland Chinese 
suppliers such as Changchuan Technology (300604 CH, NR) and JHT Design (603061

### Page 10

10

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026

While global incumbents rely on brand prestige, standard architectures, and bundled 
offerings, Hon Precision excels in leading-edge AI/HPC testing through its ultra-high-power 
Active Thermal Control (ATC) and rapid engineering turnaround. As multi-kilowatt dynamic 
loads (1.5kW–3kW+) and extreme warpage on >120mm packages push standard handlers 
beyond their limits, Hon Precision’s hyper-customised ATC fluidics, high-force micro-
mechanics, and modular designs deliver superior millisecond thermal clamping and agile 
package adaptation. Furthermore, while giants like Advantest dominate the SoC ATE tester 
market (>55% share), their in-house handlers focus primarily on memory and standard 
logic. Consequently, tier-one chipmakers and OSATs standardly decouple tester and 
handler sourcing, thus pairing Advantest or Teradyne testers with Hon Precision’s high-
power ATC handlers. Located directly within Taiwan’s advanced packaging cluster, Hon 
Precision provides real-time, on-site co-validation and custom cold-plate engineering that 
preserve formidable entry barriers against bundled solutions.

Meanwhile, although Chinese competitors have expanded aggressively in domestic mature 
nodes and conventional Pick-and-Place handlers, Hon Precision commands a decisive 
generational lead in high-complexity advanced packaging applications. Chinese vendors 
remain largely confined to lower-wattage consumer, automotive, and industrial IC testing, 
lacking the proven patents, structural rigidity, and micro-level force balancing required to 
safely apply hundreds of kilograms of contact force onto fragile, multi-chip CoWoS 
packages without cracking dies or warping substrates. Moreover, because enterprise AI 
chips cost tens of thousands of dollars each, tier-1 global fabless leaders and North 
American cloud service providers (CSPs) maintain virtually zero tolerance for testing 
failure, establishing an entrenched validation moat for Hon Precision. Free from 
geopolitical export constraints, Hon Precision can seamlessly deploy across global OSAT 
networks in Taiwan, North America, and Southeast Asia.

Financial Analysis 
AI demand to sustain strong revenue growth 
In 2025, Hon Precision’s top line exceeded TWD30bn (+116% YoY), driven by global AI 
cluster rollouts. We forecast Hon Precision to sustain its top-line growth momentum over 
2026-28E. We expect this to be mainly driven by core test handler and ATC systems (70-
75% of revenue), where we forecast capacity to expand 40% YoY in 2026E, and 50% YoY 
over 2027-28E with utilisation rate remaining above 90%. We forecast blended ASP of test 
handler ASP to rise from TWD14.2m in 2025 to TWD16.3m by 2028E, mainly driven by 
increased adoption rate of mega tray (+20-25% ASP) and AOI (+10-15%) system. High-
margin cold plate consumables revenue should also benefit from test handler shipment, 
which we forecast to increase at a 66.1% CAGR over 2025-28E.

Balance sheet and cash flow 
Driven by surging AI/HPC testing demand, Hon Precision is actively expanding its capacity 
across the new Desheng facility, localised production at the Changshu site, and 
debottlenecking of headquarter efficiency. The company targets 40-50% capacity growth in 
2026 and another 50% increase in 2027. The expansion of manufacturing space and 
production facilities requires an increase in capital expenditure, which we project Hon 
Precision will ramp up to approximately TWD2.8bn in 2026E, TWD3.8bn in 2027E, and 
TWD4.6bn in 2028E. The company’s operating cash inflows are more than sufficient to 
fully fund factory expansions alongside a c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Hon Precision continues to maintain an un-levered balance sheet with zero financial bank 
debt. Cash and cash equivalents exceeded TWD53bn in 2025; we project this figure to 
surpass TWD75bn by 2028E. Its robust balance sheet underpins peer-leading shareholder 
returns, with ROE to expand from 34.5% in 2025 to 59.6% by 2028E. This self-funding 
cash engine supports management’s targeted c.70% dividend payout ratio.

Valuation and recommendation 
Initiating coverage with a Buy (1) rating 
We initiate coverage of Hon Precision with a Buy (1) rating and 12-month TP of TWD6,365, 
based on a target PER of 30x applied to our 2027E EPS forecast. When the stock rallied 
above TWD8,000 in 1H26, its forward PER multiple stretched to a cycle peak of 45-50x. 
However, we expect key growth catalysts, including a 50% aggregate capacity expansion, 
alongside higher-ASP shipments of large-form-factor mega tray handlers and co-packaged 
optics (CPO) test solutions, to drive its top line, primarily in 2H27. With existing production 
lines operating at full utilisation, investors are concerned over a potential temporary 
deceleration in growth momentum through 2H26-1H27, ahead of the next potential major 
capacity inflection in 2H27.

However, as the calendar rolls into 2027, we believe market attention will swiftly refocus on 
the company’s multi-year structural thesis. Easing capacity bottlenecks, combined with 
robust ASIC-driven adoption of mega tray architectures and ramping CPO test platforms, 
should spark a powerful volume-and-ASP expansion cycle. The stock is currently trading at 
a 19x 1-year-forward PER vs. its earnings CAGR of 55% over 2026-28E, translating into a 
0.35x PEG ratio. We believe the valuation now heavily discounts the near-term pause in 
revenue and macro noises, presenting a compelling risk-reward profile for long-term 
investors.

Risks to our call 
1. Slower-than-expected AI/HPC investment: A slowdown in AI infrastructure spending 
or delays in GPU, ASIC and server CPU ramps could reduce demand for high-end 
FT/SLT handlers and thermal-control solutions, weakening order growth and revenue 
visibility. 
2. Slower-than-expected new product adoption: Customer qualification delays or 
slower adoption of mega-tray handlers, AOI inspection systems and CPO FT solutions 
could delay product ramps, resulting in revenue growth below our expectations. 
3. Potential share loss: Competitors’ advances in thermal control, customer qualification 
of alternative suppliers or delays in meeting new testing requirements could weaken 
Hon Precision’s position on next-generation platforms, affecting market share and 
pricing power.

### Page 15

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
Company introduction 
Hon Precision was founded in Taichung in 2015, building on the technology and industry 
experience of Hon Technology, which was established in 1999. The company develops 
semiconductor test handlers and thermal-control solutions for AI GPUs, AI ASICs, server 
CPUs, automotive chips and consumer electronics, supporting customers from new-
product introduction (NPI) through volume production.

Hon Precision has two main product categories:

• Semiconductor test handlers: Final-test (FT) and system-level-test (SLT) handlers 
automate chip loading, positioning and sorting. These platforms can incorporate active 
thermal control (ATC) systems to maintain chips at specified temperatures during 
testing. 
• Testing fixtures and modules: Mainly customised cold plates and socket layout kits 
(SLKs), which provide thermal management and mechanical interfaces for different chip 
packages. Customers may require replacement cold plates and kits as package designs 
change, even when retaining existing handlers.

Hon Precision’s order mix is significantly weighted towards AI/HPC/ASIC applications, 
which accounted for 78% of orders in 1Q-3Q26, up from 72% in 2025. These applications 
include AI computing, servers and networking. Automotive represented 9% of orders, 
followed by mobile AP and communications at 7%, consumer electronics at 4%, and 
memory and MEMS at 2%.

As for capacity, Hon Precision’s manufacturing footprint is primarily based in Taiwan, with 
Taichung serving as the group’s main R&D, engineering, quality management, and 
equipment production hub. In addition, the company operates a Suzhou facility focused on 
manufacturing cold plates and related kits, while Taichung Plant 4 is expected to provide 
incremental production capacity to support future growth.

Shareholder structure 
Hon Precision’s major shareholders include several investment and development 
companies. As of 17 April 2026, Hung Min Development was the largest shareholder with a 
17.37% stake, followed by Hongji Investment at 12.08% and Hong Yu Development at 
7.80%. Chairman Wen-Ta Hsieh also directly held 1.77%. International institutional 
shareholders included Norges Bank and GIC, with disclosed stakes of 1.30% and 0.97%, 
respectively. The company has a free float of 48%, allowing broad public-market 
participation and supporting trading liquidity.

### Page 17

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Appendix 
What is handler? 
A semiconductor test handler automates the movement, positioning and temperature 
conditioning of packaged chips during testing. It places each device into a test socket, 
applies the required contact force and sorts the device according to the test results. The 
tester performs the electrical measurements, while the handler ensures consistent 
mechanical and thermal conditions.

The process begins with packaged chips loaded into the handler in trays. After temperature 
conditioning, the handler transfers each chip into a test socket connected to the tester 
through a load board. Once electrical contact is established, the tester applies signals and 
measures the chip’s response. For ATC-equipped systems, a customised cold plate works 
with the active thermal control system to maintain the required chip temperature 
throughout testing. The handler then unloads and sorts the chips based on their test 
results.

Semiconductor test handler: workflow and thermal control system

Source: Hon Precision, Daiwa research

Handlers are used in both final test (FT) and system-level test (SLT). FT handlers work 
with automated test equipment (ATE) to check electrical characteristics and functionality 
against specifications. SLT handlers place chips into test positions connected to system-
level platforms, typically comprising test boards, test PCs and power supplies, to evaluate 
operation under conditions resembling actual use.

### Page 18

18

Hon Precision (7769 TT): 10 November 2026 
Semiconductor back-end testing FT workflow

Source: Semiconductor engineering

Thermal control becomes increasingly important as AI/HPC chips consume more power 
and adopt larger, more complex packages. Different test patterns change power 
consumption and heat distribution, requiring dynamic heating or cooling to keep the chip 
within a specified temperature range. Precise handling and temperature control support 
repeatable measurements, while parallel testing and efficient chip movement help improve 
overall test throughput.

ZDT 3Q26 core business (OPI) was 10%/11% higher than GSe and 
BBG consensus, mainly thanks to the better than expected product 
mix (high margin & high growth sectors accounted for 27% of total 
revenue in 3Q26 vs. our estimate only at 20-25%). In 3Q26, ZDT GM 
was 1.8/2.6ppt higher than GSe/BBG consensus, and was 27.7% up 
4.6ppt QoQ and 5.7ppt YoY, while the annual growth was only 
driven by the better product mix/ new business contribution 
(ABF/optical module/AI PCB), even considering these new 
businesses only accounted for 27% of total revenue.  
OPEX in 3Q26 increased 24% QoQ and 56% YoY due mainly to the 
higher investment in the R&D projects for future new high-end AI 
PCB/ optical transceiver / substrates business, which was the main 
reason for the company to see a 0.7/1.4ppt higher than GSe/BBG 
consensus OPEX to sales ratio in 3Q26.  
In 4Q26, we expect ZDT revenue to go up by ~29% QoQ, mainly due 
to the solid growth from the optical transceiver, substrates, and AI 
PCB businesses, with more new AI projects to ramp in 4Q26. 
Moreover, we expect the company’s GM to further expand to ~30% 
(2.2ppt QoQ or 7.3ppt YoY) thanks to a better product mix (we 
expect high growth/high margin new business will account for 
30-35% of the company’s total revenue in 4Q26, whose average GM 
should be at least 40-45% in 4Q26).  
Investment view 
We remain positive on ZDT’s AI business outlook, and expect the 
acceleration on ZDT’s AI PCB/Substrates business revenue to further 
drive the company’s overall GM level in coming years (we expect the 
two businesses’ average GM could surpass 50% in 2027, thanks to 
the favorable supply demand condition) while we also expect the 
high GM optical transceiver (50%+ GM now) will also see at least 
120% YoY growth in 2027, suggesting a significant GM expansion 
trend for the overall company in coming quarters/years.

e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

We believe ZDT will continue to be the key share gainer in the AI PCB market. For AI GPU 
projects, we are seeing ZDT share increase from <5% in 2025 to ~10-15% in 2026, 
which, we believe, will further go up to 30%+ in long term (2028 and beyond), thanks to 
the company’s proactive investment in the R&D on the latest PCB technology, as well as 
the company’s solid investment in the AI grade PCB capacity. For AI ASIC projects, the 
company’s market share is well below 10% for now, and only focus on some minor 
projects, but we do expect the company to become one of the major suppliers for AI 
ASIC projects in coming years, considering the company’s more diversified production 
sites (mainland China + Taiwan + Thailand), while most of the AI ASIC customers are 
asking for non-Greater China capacity now.  
For the optical transceiver business, we expect ZDT to see strong growth (50%+ 
2025-29E CAGR), driven mainly by the ~50% 2026-28E volume demand CAGR and the 
company’s proactive expansion in capacity in coming years (5x more capacity over the 
next two years). Moreover, ZDT currently holds an industry-leading yield of 90%+ for its 
optical module mSAP products (vs. peers only at 60-80% per our industry checks).  
For its ABF substrate business, we believe the company will continue to have the ability 
to increase ABF price, supported by full UTR and strong demand, while the industry in 
our view remains conservative on its capacity expansion plans over the next 1-2 years 
relative to our forecasts. We expect ABF substrate LTA/Spot prices to increase by 
10-15%/20%+ QoQ per quarter from 3Q26 through at least the end of 2027, with 
shortages likely extending into 2028 (see here). Overall, the business condition for ABF 
substrate is improving with price increase and we expect the business, which was loss 
making in 2025, to expand its GM to 40%+/50%+ by end of 2026/27. 
Overall, we reiterate our Buy rating on ZDT, with a new 12m TP of NT$1,070 (from 
NT$1,025).

Earnings revisions 
We revise up our 2026/27/28/29E net income estimate by 6/21/42/49% after seeing 
the solid GM expansion from these high-growth sectors, which we believe will continue 
to be the net income driver in the coming years. We believe the high growth sectors (AI 
PCB, substrates, optical module mSAP) will account for 50%+ of total revenue in 2028 
and further expand to 60%+ in 2029, with a much higher than company average GM 
(50%+ in average in 2027-28), thus we revise up our 2027/28/29E GM by 
5.4/10.7/12.4ppt vs. previous estimate.

### Page 4

Valuation 
We revise up ZDT 12m TP from NT$1,025 to NT$1,070, still based on our SOTP based 
valuation methodology of (1) 3.5x blended P/B for ABF & BT substrate (in-line with 
upcycle industry average), (2) 15x P/E for high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in 
line with industry average), and (3) 8x P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB 
(in-line with industry downcycle average valuation), and maintain our valuation period at 
4Q26-3Q27, factoring in our earnings revision.

Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in 
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in 
Shenzhen, China, and has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market 
compared to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% 
ABF substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the 
most from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025E-27E CAGR) 
due to the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. 
We also expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to 
drive ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 50% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We 
see further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due 
to our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which 
should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long 
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth 
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+ 
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China 
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Auras Technology (3324.TWO)

Auras Technology
Valuation
We value Auras using a PE-based approach, reflecting its accelerating earnings trajectory and rising share in liquid cooling
market of AI servers. Our TP of NT$2,000 is based on 20x 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified given the company’s
expanding server revenue base, deeper engagement with CSP customers, and broader product portfolio across cold plates,
CDMs, and CDUs.

Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model, but assigning a High Risk rating is not supported in our view by
other qualitative factors such as rapid RVL progress and Thailand expansion, and so a High Risk rating has not been applied.
Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price include: 1) slower adoption of liquid cooling; 2) heightened price
competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

大量(3167 TT) Buy

CPO 設備將於 3Q27 量產，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
投資建議：大量受惠於 AI 帶動 PCB 製造、半導體檢測需求，
獲利大幅成長，CPO 設備將於 3Q27 量產，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
大量受惠於 AI 伺服器帶動高階 PCB 鑽孔設備：大量在±2 mil 
PCB 背鑽計量領域的地位依然穩固；大量受惠於 AI 伺服器帶
動高階 PCB 鑽孔設備需求的趨勢，而非單純受惠某一客戶的拉
貨。 
3Q26 毛利率季增約 6pts： 大量 3Q26 營收 36.71 億元，
QoQ+28.40%；稅後純益 8.99 億元，QoQ+65.51%；稅後 EPS 9.85
元。 
3Q26 毛利率季增約 6pts，主要來自產品組合優化，包含高階
CCD 機種及 TM3、 TM4。一般機種毛利率約 20~30%，高階機
種毛利率約 50%， TM3、 TM4 毛利率更高，TM4 於 3Q26 出貨
約 5 台。 
大量的子公司-大量先進智能規劃 IPO：大量先進智能已經於
08/01/2026 獨立，目前大量持股大量先進智能 100%，預計大量
先進智能上櫃後，大量持股大量先進智能仍會超過 60%。  
大量先進智能的核心技術包含了半導體的精密量測
(Metrology)、 AOI 智慧檢測(Inspection)及 (Automation)自動化。
產品應用包含 CoWoS、 SoIC 及面板級封裝(FOPLP)(CoPoS)、
CPO 等。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
E：(Environmental)。大量科技秉持「愛護地球、永續發展」之理念，持續推
動環境管理制度，並透過日常營運管理落實環境保護與污染預防，降低營運
活動對環境造成之影響。大量科技秉持「愛護地球、永續發展」之理念，持
續推動環境管理制度，並透過日常營運管理落實環境保護與污染預防，降低
營運活動對環境造成之影響。大量科技針對廠區內原物料、製程、生產活動
及服務建立環境考量評估機制，評估範圍涵蓋正常、異常及緊急狀況下可能
產生之環境影響，並納入空氣排放、水污染、廢棄物、能源使用、土地污染、
噪音及其他環境衝擊等項目。 
S：(Social)。大量科技重視人才發展與員工照顧，將員工視為公司穩健營運
與永續經營的重要基礎。大量科技秉持以人為本的精神，致力於培育與留任
專業人才，並透過多元招募、薪酬福利、教育訓練、勞資溝通及權益保障等
制度，持續打造友善、平等且具支持性的職場環境。大量科技於人才任用上
遵循相關勞動法令及多元平等原則，重視員工專業能力發展及工作權益保
障，並持續透過教育訓練提升員工技能，強化員工與公司共同成長之基礎。
G：(Governance)。大量科技致力於推動產品朝高規格、高自動化、高智能化
及完全整合化之方向發展，以貼近客戶需求，並與客戶共同開發新產品。此
外，公司持續投入自動化設備之研發，厚植核心技術能力，並持續開發進階
產品，以掌握市場先機。為確保制度有效運作，大量科技於企劃室下設企業
誠信經營小組，由總經理擔任召集人，負責誠信經營政策之推動與執行情形
之監督，並每年向董事會報告執行成果及防範不誠信行為之措施。大量科技
依董事會通過之「誠信經營守則」及相關內部規章，建立對於不誠信行為之
相關防範機制，並透過內部控制、檢舉制度及教育訓練等措施，確保誠信經
營政策之落實。 
公司簡介： 
大量的產主要產品應用於製造、半導體先進製程與先進封裝，以及光電、玻
璃基板與精密材料加工等領域。透過自主研發之控制器、應用軟體、視覺定
位、深度控制、運動控制及量測檢測技術，大量科技依客戶製程需求提供設
備及技術服務。 
PCB 設備：PCB 鑽孔定位、PCB 成型切割、邊緣滾塗、針塗等塗佈製程及
高速型內層銅深度量測機等。 
半導體設備：CMP Pad 即時量測監控設備、3D IC 先進封裝製程晶圓及晶片
量測設備、FOPLP 形貌量測設備、3DIC 先進封裝製程檢測設備、Flip Chip 
製程檢測設備、捲帶包裝晶片本體及外觀缺陷之檢查設備等、封裝製程自動
化設備等。 
大量 PCB 客戶的客戶包含台灣、中國的 PCB 廠；半導體設備客戶包含晶圓
代工廠、OSAT。  
高階背鑽設備的市場，主要同業為德國 Schmoll Maschinen。  
資料來源：大量年報。

4 群益投顧 
PCB 設備包含鑽孔機、成型機和塗膠設備。鑽孔設備也包含量測設備(TM3、
TM4 系列，應用於背鑽檢測)。  
大量有找外包廠商來增加產能： 
考量標準產品與高階產品，大量目前自有的月產能約 400 台，預計到 2027
年每月會達 500 台，大量會再增加外包的產能約 100~150 台。 
大量的子公司-大量先進智能規劃 IPO：  
大量先進智能已經於 08/01/2026 獨立，目前大量持股大量先進智能 100%，
預計大量先進智能未來上櫃後，大量持股大量先進智能仍會超過 60%。  
大量先進智能的核心技術包含了半導體的精密量測(Metrology)、AOI 智慧檢
測 (Inspection)及 (Automation)自動化。產品應用包含 CoWoS、 SoIC 及面板
級封裝(FOPLP)(CoPoS)、 CPO 等。

資料來源：大量法說簡報。 
2027 年 PCB 與半導體設備展望皆正向： 
PCB 設備 2027 年每月都有要交機，交期約 5~6 月；半導體設備 B/B ratio 維
持在 2 以上。 
大量預估 2027 年 CCD 成型機與鑽孔機將維持雙位數成長，TM3 完成階段
性任務後出貨將減少，TM4 出貨量預期會有大幅倍數成長。PCB 在手訂單
有達千台。

8 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

## Document 215: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정삼성전자(005930) 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정 - 기업.pdf

Date: 2026-10-07

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%)과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%)을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

## Document 231: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한_07bab1f0

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락두산에너빌리티(034020) 펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한_07bab1f0.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(034020) 
두산에너빌리티 
2026.10.30

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

펀더멘털은 견고, 노이즈로 인한 일시적 주가 하락 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 80,000 원(하향) 
 현재주가 56,700 원(10/29)

 [투자의견] 당사는 두산에너빌리티에 대해 목표주가를 80,000원(2027년 
BPS의 6.2배)으로 27% 하향하며, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 실적은 견조
한 회복을 보이고 있으나, 최근 GE Vernova(GEV) 등 글로벌 AI 및 에너지 
밸류체인 Peer 주가 조정을 반영해 멀티플을 하향 조정했다. 체코 시공 계
약 등 일부 노이즈에 의해 주가가 급락한 만큼 매수 기회라고 판단한다.  
 [3Q26 Review] 매출액 4.4조원(-5.8%qoq, +14.7%yoy), 영업이익 
2,612억원(-16.9%qoq, +90.6%yoy)을 기록해 당사 추정에 부합하는 무
난한 실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티 부문은 매출액 1.8조원, 영업이익 1,211
억원(영업이익률 6.6%, +2.3%p qoq)을 기록하며 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 
고수익 사업 믹스 개선으로 분기 수익성이 재차 개선됐다. 당기순이익은 
149억원(-93.4%qoq)에 그쳤으나, 원달러 환율 급락으로 발생한 환손실 
반영 때문으로 이익 체력과는 무관하다고 판단한다. 3Q26 미국 빅테크 데
이터센터향 가스터빈 첫 출하를 달성했으며, 수주잔고는 에너빌리티 부문 
기준 26.6조원(+70.9%yoy)으로 사상 최대치를 경신했다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 4.8조원, 영업이익 2,640억원으로 유사한 실적 
흐름을 전망한다. 4Q 가스터빈 인도와 체코 두코바니 원전 진행률 반영으로 
에너빌리티 부문 외형 성장이 기대된다. 3Q26 별도 기준 영업이익률이 
8.5%까지 개선된 만큼 에너빌리티 부문의 분기 실적 개선 추세는 유효하다
고 판단한다. 연간 수주 목표 13.3조원 달성을 위한 잔여 수주(3.3조원 +α)
가 예정되어 있으며, 대형원전을 제외하더라도 가이던스 달성은 무난할 것
이라 판단한다. 세부적으로는 SMR 1조원(계약 조건 합의 완료 단계로 연내 
체결 가능), 신재생 7,000억원(해상풍력 기자재·EPC 2건 협의 완료 단계), 
해외 복합 EPC 5,000억원(국내 1건, 해외 2건)이 기대된다. 
 [가스터빈] 3Q26 누적 가스터빈 13기·스팀터빈 11기·LTSA 6건을 달성하
며 연간 10기 이상 수주·수행 역량이 안정적으로 유지되고 있다. 2030년 
슬롯 예약 물량의 본계약 전환은 2027년, 2031년 슬롯은 2028년으로 예
상되며, 수주잔고 증가가 기대된다. 가스터빈 수요 증가를 감안하면 추가 캐
파 증설 결정이 이르면 연내 이루어질 전망이다. 
 [원전] 10월 NuScale 1개 모듈 제작 계약을 체결했으며, PPA 지연 여부와 
무관하게 완결된 계약으로 실적에 반영될 전망이다. X-energy는 연내 기자
재 및 부품의 후속 계약을 추진 중이다. TerraPower 주기기 3종 중 2종 계
약이 완료되어 제작에 착수했으며, Rolls-Royce SMR·i-SMR 등 파트너십 
확대도 지속되고 있다. 이외에 대미투자 관련 기자재 발주는 미국 부지 확정 
및 한국 의회 승인 절차가 남아있어 내년 발주가 기대된다. 기존 웨스팅하우
스 AP1000 Vogtle 계약 대비 업무 범위 확대(원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 
품목) 가능성이 있어 긍정적으로 평가한다.

### Page 2

두산에너빌리티

2_ Eugene Research Center 
설명회 Q&A 
Q1. 4Q26 SMR 수주 목표 약 1 조원의 실현 가능성 
- 고객사와 GTC 등 주요 계약 조건에 대한 합의를 대부분 완료하고 계약 체결 대기 중 
- 연내 계약 가능성이 있어 수주 목표는 의미 있는 수준

Q2. NuScale 모듈 1 기 제작 계약의 PPA 지연 영향 
- 완결된 제작 계약으로 PPA 체결이 지연되더라도 해당 모듈의 제작 및 진행에는 문제없음

Q3. AP1000 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 회계 처리 및 사업 영향 
- DOE·DOC 및 한국 정부 재원을 활용해 EPC 계약·착공 지연과 무관하게 기자재 제작 가능 
- 프로젝트 중단 시 웨스팅하우스의 다른 프로젝트로 기자재 전용 가능성 검토

Q4. 체코 원전 컨소시엄 변경 시 참여 및 사업 계획 
- 현지 지점 운영과 시공인력 상주로 시공 환경 자료 확보 
- 한수원과 초기 인허가·설계에 참여하고, 주기기 공급·설치 역량을 바탕으로 경쟁력 확보 기대

Q5. 체코 원전 EPC 사업의 수익성 확보 가능성 
- 한수원 가이드에 따라 수익성을 고려한 입찰 진행 예정

Q6. 웨스팅하우스 SPV 및 폴란드 AP1000 장주기 기자재 발주 진행 상황 
- DOE·DOC 프로젝트와 폴란드 AP1000 3 기 기자재 조기 발주 협의 중 
- 연내 수주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 수주 추진

Q7. 3Q26 영업이익률 개선 요인 
- 원자력·가스터빈 중심의 사업 믹스 개선이 수익성에 기여 
- 제작 진행률과 마일스톤에 따라 이익 인식

### Page 3

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _3 
Q8. 4Q26 복합화력 수주 목표 5,000 억원의 달성 가능성 
- 국내 1 건, 해외 2 건 협의 중이며 국내 프로젝트의 수주 가능성이 높은 상황 
- 대미 투자 물량은 기존 2031 년까지의 예약계약 납기와 비교해 검토 중

Q9. 3Q26 에너빌리티부문 당기순손실 원인 
- 급격한 환율 하락에 따른 약 800 억원의 환산손실이 주요 원인

Q10. 대미 투자 관련 기자재 발주 시점 
- 미국 부지 확정, 웨스팅하우스 사업제안 및 한국 정부 심사·국회 승인 절차 필요 
- 연말~내년 초 발주 여부는 불확실하나 조기 발주 추진

Q11. 대미 투자 프로젝트의 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 원자로·증기발생기 외 추가 품목까지 공급 범위 확대를 위해 웨스팅하우스와 협상 중

Q12. 장주기 기자재 선발주 후 인허가 지연 시 실적 영향 
- 기자재 출하 시 수금 조건이 충족되며 선발주 자금도 사전 확보 
- 보글·VC 서머 사례에서 기자재 대금을 회수해 프로젝트 지연에 따른 실적 영향은 제한적

Q13. 4Q26 신재생·풍력 수주 목표 약 7,000 억원의 달성 가능성 
- 해상풍력 기자재·EPC 계약 및 유지보수 계약 추진 중 
- 주요 계약 협의가 상당 부분 완료돼 일정 지연이 없다면 연내 목표 달성 전망

Q14. 중형 가스터빈 개발 및 해외 진출 계획 
- 대형 가스터빈은 한국·미국 중심으로 추진하며 중동 고객사와도 협의 중 
- 휴스턴 서비스 허브를 기반으로 미국 데이터센터 시장 대응 
- 중형 가스터빈은 시제품 제작 및 시험 진행 후 2028 년 하반기 출하 목표

### Page 4

두산에너빌리티

4_ Eugene Research Center 
Q15. NuScale 모듈 1 기 선발주 배경 
- 대규모 일괄 발주에 대비한 제작 경험 확보 및 납기 리스크 완화를 위해 사전 제작 추진

Q16. X-energy 연내 후속 계약 대상 품목 
- 증기발생기 전열관 및 주단 소재 등 장주기 품목의 사전 발주 추진

Q17. DOE 프로젝트의 장주기 기자재 공급 범위 확대 가능성 
- 웨스팅하우스 공급망 상황에 따라 추가 품목 협상 중 
- 과거 보글·VC 서머보다 넓은 공급 범위 기대

Q18. 웨스팅하우스 지분 투자와 기자재 계약 시점의 연계 가능성 
- 지분 투자는 내년, 기자재 계약은 연내 또는 내년 초 목표로 시점이 일치할지는 불확실

Q19. X-energy 장주기 기자재의 소재·부품 구분 
- 증기발생기 전열관은 부품, 주단 소재는 소재에 해당 
- 전열관은 공급 병목에 대비해 조기 발주하고 주단 소재는 설계 확정 후 제작 준비

Q20. 복합화력 주기기 수주의 기존·신규 고객 비중 
- 기존 고객과 추가 예약계약을 협의 중이며 신규 글로벌 고객과도 예약계약 협상 진행

Q21. 주요 SMR 사업 외 파트너십 확대 가능성 
- Rolls-Royce 사업 추진 및 GE Hitachi BWRX-300 주단 소재 예약계약 체결 
- i-SMR 은 2035 년 운전을 목표로 2028 년 이전 발주가 필요해 관련 협의 진행

Q22. X-energy 전열관·주단 소재 수주 물량 규모 
- 다우케미컬 초도 사업은 80MW 원자로 4 개 모듈로 구성되며, 해당 4 개 모듈분 부품·소재 공급

### Page 5

두산에너빌리티

Eugene Research Center _5 
Q23. 중동 지정학적 불안정에 따른 프로젝트·물류 영향 
- 기존 프로젝트의 운송비 증가분을 발주처·개발사와 협의 중 
- 신규 계약에는 비용 보상 및 공기 지연 관련 조항 반영

Q24. 가스터빈 생산능력 증설 규모 및 시기 
- 현재 생산능력 증설 중이며 2031 년 출하분까지 예약 접수 
- 시장 수요에 따라 추가 증설 검토

Q25. 2030 년 가스터빈 예약 물량의 실제 계약 전환 시점 
- 2030 년 출하 물량은 대부분 내년 실제 계약 여부 결정 전망 
- 2031 년 출하 물량은 2028 년경 계약 여부 결정 예상

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12 개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

ㆍBUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +15%이상 ~ +50%미만 94% 
ㆍHOLD(중립) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%이상 ∼ +15%미만 3%

## Document 245: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적HD현대중공업(329180) 우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
우려 감안해도 충분히 매력적 
2026-10-14

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 820,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 366,500 원 
상승여력: 123.7%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 및 환율 영향으로 시장 예상치 하회 예상 
Key: 4Q26 부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 계약 수주 모멘텀 기대 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 4 행정 엔진&FDC 으로 주가 상승여력 
충분. 매수

3Q26 Preview: 컨센서스 하회하는 실적 기록 예상 
3Q26 연결 매출액 6조 464억원(+36.9% YoY, -4.5% QoQ), 영업이익 9,230억
원(+65.6% YoY, -11.2% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(1조 70억원)를 하회하는 실적 기
록할 것으로 추정한다. 이전 분기 대비 중대재해 및 하계휴가, 그리고 추석 연휴에 따른 
조업일수 감소 효과와 더불어 큰 폭의 환율 하락 영향(약 500 억원) 때문이다. 홍범도
함 화재 및 임단협 관련 일회성 비용은 이번 분기가 아닌 4Q 에 반영될 것으로 예상한
다. 해양 부문에서 트리온 FPU 프로젝트 C/O 에 따른 일부 환입 발생한 것으로 추정
되며, 해양 도크에서 컨테이너선 선체 블록 일부 건조로 고정비 감소효과 일부 상쇄 중
이다. 다만 올해 연내 혹은 내년 초 추가 해양 수주 없을 시 고정비 상승 우려 확대될 
전망이다. 동사 올해 상선 수주 목표(114.7 억불)를 이미 초과하는 수주 실적 기록한 상
황이며, 고마진 선종인 LNGC 및 VLGC/VLAC 중심으로 수주했기 때문에 ‘28~’29
년 구간 경쟁사 대비 뚜렷한 선종 믹스 효과를 기대한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 82 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 97 만원에서 82
만원으로 하향한다. 중국의 공격적인 캐파 증설 지속과 더불어 중국 대형 조선사들의 
글로벌 선주가 발주한 LNGC 물량 및 원가 경쟁력 확대에 따른 상선 부문의 멀티플 
조정이 불가피하다는 판단이다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클
과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지
속되고 있는 중으로 파악된다. 올해 4 분기부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 부문의 본격적
인 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 전망이며, 해당 모멘텀에 따른 주가 상승
여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)를 유지한다.

Key points
• The Cotality home value index – covering
all of Australia – fell 1.2% in October
following a 1.3% decline in September
and 1.4% falls in August and July.

Annual growth has turned negative,
running at –2.8%yr with prices down
6.7% from their peak in March.
• Across the major cities, Brisbane and
Perth led the declines in the October
month, recording falls of 2.0% and
1.7% respectively, but the correction
remains more pronounced in Sydney
(prices down a further 1.6%mth to be
off 9.4%yr) with Melbourne also off
more sharply over the year (–7.6%yr)
but recording a milder October
(–0.8%mth). Adelaide remains relatively

resilient, recording a 1.2% decline in
the month but prices still up 3.7%yr.
• Preliminary estimates show the
number of sales, in rolling 3mth
terms, down 5.6%qtr and 22.5%yr.

### Page 2

forecasts are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The Cotality home value index, covering all of Australia, fell
1.2% in October with slight revisions to previous months
again shaving a further 0.3% off prices. The correction
has now seen prices decline 6.7% from their March peak

with annual growth firmly in negative at –2.8%yr. Turnover
continues fall as well, down 22.5%yr. Housing-related
tax policy changes, interest rate rises and heightened
uncertainty have triggered a sharp contraction in markets.
The downturn is now comparable to the previous two
housing market corrections. Nationally, the downturn in
2022-23 saw prices decline 7.5% and turnover fall 29% peak
to trough. The 2017-18 correction saw prices fall 8.6% and

turnover by 24%, with larger falls in Sydney and Melbourne.
Note that unless specified, figures in titles, tables and
commentary are in non‑seasonally adjusted terms, consistent
with the ‘headline’ figures reported by Cotality. However,
figures shown in charts are in seasonally adjusted terms.
Preliminary estimates of turnover show rolling 3mth volumes
down 5.6%qtr in October, previous estimates again revised
lower. The monthly pattern suggests declines re‑accelerated

through September‑October after stabilising somewhat in July‑
August. The RBA's interest rate rise in late September and a
further expected tightening likely contributed to the shift.
The detail shows Brisbane and Perth are now leading the
way in terms of monthly price declines although corrections
to date remain significantly larger in Sydney and Melbourne
while smaller capitals and regional areas continue to see much
milder downturns. Across price tiers, all segments are now

showing similar rates of decline after top price tiers had seen
a more pronounced and earlier downturn. 'Top tier' segments
have tended to lead recent price cycles.
Looking across jurisdictions,
• Sydney prices dropped 1.6% in October having averaged
similar‑sized declines over the previous five months. While
all segments and tiers continue to record falls, the last few
months has seen the pace of decline moderate a touch in the
top tier segments that have led cycles in the past, although

they are still a long way from stabilisation.
• Melbourne prices declined 0.8%mth, in line with
September's move and continuing the slower pace of
retreat. As noted, this likely reflects the weaker starting
point coming into the correction, prices already at 5½ year
lows. Interestingly, the detail shows the moderation in price
declines in recent months has been more pronounced for
'top tier' units where prices are back where they were in
2016.
Housing downturn deepens

### Page 3

forecasts are based are reasonable, the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially
from these forecasts.
3WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
• Brisbane prices are tracking a very fast rate of decline
that is also seeing large downward revisions. Prices fall
2% in October with September and August now both
showing 2.1% falls, revised down from 1.5% and 1.6%

respectively (August having previously been estimated at
just 1%, July's move having seen a similar series of revisions
–1.2% to –1.8%). The combined effect has taken the rolling
3mth change to –6.1%qtr from –4.7%qtr last months and
–2.7%qtr in August and –0.6%qtr in July. At 0.4%yr, annual
price growth has virtually stalled flat having run at 18%yr as
recently as May. All tiers and segments are recording similar
rates of decline.
• Perth is seeing something very similar, prices dropping a

further 1.7% in October, with September's decline revised
down to 2.1% from –1.2% and August's to 2.0% from –1.7%.
At 3.9%yr, annual growth is still positive but off steeply
from the 24% pace back in May. As with Brisbane, all tiers
and segments are recording similar rates of decline.
• Adelaide dwelling prices posted a somewhat milder 1.2%
decline in October, , September's –1.3% decline marked
down to –1.7%. Annual price growth slowed to 3.7%yr,

similar to Perth's but with less of a whiplash from strong
gains to large declines. Turnover has also been much
steadier, sales holding flat over the last three months and
down just 12.4%yr.
• The smaller capitals were again more mixed, price growth
still positive in Darwin (0.6%mth, 9.7%yr), relatively resilient
in Hobart (+0.5%mth, 5.5%yr) but weaker in Canberra
(–0.7%mth, –3.7%yr).
• Regional markets showed a 0.9% decline in Qld but slightly
milder declines in NSW (–0.7%) and Victoria (0.6%). Annual

prices growth is in the 1.4‑3.5%yr range.
All up, the October updates make for bleak reading. With the
RBA widely expected to raise interest rates again, the near
term outlook remains very challenging. Next week's consumer
sentiment update will show how this is shaping housing‑
related sentiment – so far, consumer price expectations have
weakened but stopped short of swinging to the clear negative
consensus that emerged in the 2022‑23 correction. That now

looks to be at risk. Either way, momentum looks set to remain
weak into year‑end and, barring a significantly improved
inflation and interest rate outlook, well into 2027.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
3
OVERVIEW
Coming into the home strait for 2026,
the Australian consumer has again
got the staggers. Spending has held
up surprisingly well given the intense
headwinds that have buffeted household
budgets over the year. But the same cannot
be said for sentiment which has fallen off
badly in the face of more shocks.

Our October Red Book surveys the scene.
Psychologically, consumers has seen a
return to the ‘cost of living nightmare’
they struggled with through much of
2022, 2023 and 2024. Global forces
and domestic factors are again having
pervasive negative impacts on budgets.
The scale may not be as severe as the
previous episode – inflation is running in
the 3-4% range not the 7-8% and interest
rates have risen 1ppt not 4 – but the
feeling is similar. In part this because there

has was very little reprieve in between –
despite tax cuts and a partial unwind of the
interest rate tightening in 2025, household
incomes never got a chance to regain the
lost ground.
The inflation ‘relapse’, and the difficulty
the RBA and governments seem to be
having in managing the situation, is likely
feeding into fears about the outlook for the
wider economy as well.
Exactly what this means for spending is
not entirely clear. As discussed in our last

report, sentiment reads have been much
weaker than actual spending in recent
years. The apparent disconnect is likely
due to a mix of legacy effects from COVID,
and the specific challenges posed by high
inflation (including the lingering effects
of high prices even once inflation has
subsided).
On balance we expect the spending side
to slow materially, particularly as we
head into year end. Indeed, the seven
weeks between now and Christmas
could prove decisive for the consumer

outlook, beginning with another important
sentiment survey update and then the high
season for retail. How spending performs
outside of key sales windows will be most
telling.
Staggering into year end

1. Consumer spending: slowdown ahead
“... sentiment ... has fallen off badly in the face of
more shocks”

### Page 4

or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
4
The Westpac–Melbourne Institute
Consumer Sentiment Index declined 4.2%
over the three months to Oct, unwinding a
mini-rally through the middle of the year.
At 80.4 the average read over the Oct
survey implies that pessimists outnumber
optimists by nearly 20%, a deeply
pessimistic level by historical standards

that is amongst the forty worst reads since
the survey began in the 1973.
The survey detail shows concerns are still
primarily about the cost-of-living and high
interest rates than the more acute job and
insolvency problems that arise during a
recession. The RBA’s Sep rate hike, mid-
way through the survey week looks to have
badly rattled consumers.
The component detail shows a broad
pull-back but with bigger declines in
assessments of family finances and

buyer sentiment and milder declines in
assessments of the economic outlook.
Consumers remain intensely risk averse.
The Westpac Risk Aversion Index held near
historical highs in Sep, readings still only a
touch of the 52-year high of 60.8 recorded
in June 2023. Recall that the index is based
on responses to questions on the ‘wisest
place for savings’. Responses show ‘safe
options’ remain very heavily favoured.
The sentiment mix points to a significant

weakening in spending momentum is
weakening. CSI±, a modified indicator
that shows a better correlation with per
capita spending, points to a shift back to
significant outright contractions in per
capita spending. That said, spending
in 2026 has held up better than these
indicators have suggested so far.
Consumers are on high alert for interest
rate. The Westpac–Melbourne Institute
Mortgage Rate Expectations Index rose
5.5% to 179 over the three months to

Oct, just shy of May’s cycle peak of 181.
The RBA has raised interest rates 25bps
since then. The Nov survey update due Nov
10 will be an important update on how
expectations have evolved.
The ‘time to buy a major item’ index
dropped 4.3% over the last three months,
taking it back near the lows seen earlier
in the year and a full 40pts below the
long run avg. Durables spend has been
mixed over the first half of 2026, posting a
surprisingly solid gain in Q2 but with most

of this centred on new vehicle spend where
‘extenuating circumstances’ were at play.
Homebuyer sentiment has been
understandably unsettled in recent months
given heightened uncertainty around
interest rate and tax policy changes amid
evidence of a broadening downturn in
established markets.
The ‘time to buy a dwelling’ index actually
lifted 3.6% over the three months to Oct
but is still at firmly pessimistic levels. Both
monthly reads and sub-group detail have

been very unsettled.
The broadening and deepening downturn
in Australia’s housing markets is seeing
an equally rapid shift in consumer house
price expectations although these have not
turned as negative as they did during the
2022-23 correction.
The Westpac–Melbourne Institute
Consumer House Price Expectations Index
declined a further 2.4% over the 3mths to
Oct, following a steep 25% drop over the
previous 3mths. At 115.6, the Oct index
read is miles below the 15yr high of 174 at

the start of the year and below the long run
avg of 130. That said, the read is still above
100, meaning more consumers expect
prices to rise than fall.
The labour market remains an island of
relative calm for consumers. The Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Unemployment
Expectations Index rose 9.3% over the
three months to Oct, reversing most of
the 12.1% improvement over the previous
three months (recall that higher reads mean
more consumers expect unemployment to

rise over the year ahead). At 142.2, the Oct
index read is now decisively above the long
run avg of 129 again but  still well below
the levels that would sound more worrying
alarms.
As noted, the Nov survey, currently in the
field and due to be released on Nov 10,
will provide critical updates on many of
these themes.
Quick run-down

### Page 5

or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
5
THE CONSUMER MOOD
• Consumers remain stuck in a cost-of-
living nightmare that seems to have
not end in sight. Sentiment remains
deeply pessimistic, weighed down by
continued uncertainty about the path
for inflation and interest rates, the
latter providing a clear negative ‘jolt’
midway through the Oct survey week.

• The Westpac Melbourne Institute
Index of Consumer Sentiment declined
4.2% over the three months to Oct,
unwinding a mini-rally through the
middle of the year. At 80.4 the average
read over the Oct survey implies that
pessimists outnumber optimists by
nearly 20%, a deeply pessimistic level
by historical standards. It is amongst
the forty worst reads since the survey
began in the 1973. We have had
two others in this unfortunate group
already this year (in Apr and Jun) with

another eight in 2022–23. Altogether
this marks the worst period of recurring
extremely weak sentiment since the
disastrous recession in the early 1990s.
• The survey detail shows consumer
concerns are still primarily about the
cost-of-living and high interest rates
than the more acute job and insolvency
problems that arise during a recession.
• Average weekly pump prices pushed
back over $2.30/litre nationally in early
Oct, rising to over $2.35/litre since. This

is near the peaks seen back in April and
up nearly 25% since the start of the
year. Meanwhile the RBA interest rate
rise in late Sep took the cash rate to
4.6%, the highest since 2011, with the
standard variable mortgage interest
rate pushing above 9% for the first time
since 2008.
• The RBA’s Sep move badly rattled
consumers. Sentiment amongst the
60% of the sample surveyed prior came
in at 86.9, up slightly from the Sep
read. The sentiment read across the

40% surveyed after the RBA decision
came in at just 67.2, an alarmingly weak
read that, for complete surveys, has
only been registered during the depths
of the early 1990s recession. The nearly
20% drop between pre and post RBA
samples is also the largest negative
swing we have seen since we started
tracking daily responses in 2019.
• The RBA has delivered another hike as
we go to press, meaning Nov is likely
to see yet another interest rate hike

to sentiment (the survey result will be
released on Nov 10).

3. Consumer sentiment: finances, economic conditions
